THE CITY · Llama 70B, rules v2, 400 days, game 1
Nobody proposes a tax, so the city store never holds food. Three founders starve by day 79; the relief rules passed on days 81 and 339 promise food that is never there.
24 founders · 400 days · with a town hall · seed 1 · llama
